Second, domain-gap-agnostic initialization: applying a single fixed source model uniformly across all target domains ignores domain gap severity, causing premature overfitting to noisy pseudo-labels under large shifts. We propose DGA-CAFC to address both failures. The Counterfactual Attention-based Feature Consistency (CAFC) module enforces feature stability by minimizing the discrepancy between representations computed under learned attention and those under counterfactual-inspired normalized perturbations, thereby promoting domain-robust features independent of spurious attention shifts. The Domain-Gap Aware Source Model Selection (DGASS) module estimates domain gap severity via target prediction entropy and selects the source model variant whose confidence level best matches the estimated gap, reducing negative transfer and stabilizing pseudo-label quality. Crucially, CAFC transfers beyond SFDA: applied as a lightweight regularizer to standard domain generalization objectives without target data. Extensive experiments across SFDA and DG benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently improves over recent baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness and generality of the proposed framework. Project page is available at https://visdomlab.github.io/DGA_CAFC

</details>


### [140] [Beyond Covers and Titles: Evidence-First Ranking for Robust Cold-Start Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26249)
*Fuyi Wang,Yuxin Cao,Yifan Liao,Renyang Liu,Lin Li,Wei Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示短视频内容塔仅凭封面/标题等"包装"即可操纵冷启动排名，提出可实现的REAP攻击，并据此设计证据优先排序EFR防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对内容感知推荐系统的攻击只扰动像素或特征且忽略平台重编码，防御则基于范数有界扰动，既不匹配真实编辑又损害推荐质量；同时冷启动排名对"包装"的依赖性未被系统研究。

Method: 1) REAP：经模拟平台信道（H.264转码、缩放、裁剪、JPEG）施加真实可实现的包装编辑攻击；2) 观察：仅看视频本体（body）的内容塔冷启动排序最佳；3) EFR：以视频本体为主评分冷视频，包装仅通过受γ约束的支持门控残差引入，并按排名百分位合并冷/热排名，给出对任意包装编辑的精确证书。

Result: REAP使冷视频在无关受众中排名百分位提升32.0个点；MicroLens-100K上EFR将攻击增益削减81%，冷启动Recall@100由0.1133升至0.170，合并后NDCG@10与无防御系统差距在4%以内。

Conclusion: 冷启动排名不应依赖包装信息；证据优先的架构可在保持推荐质量的同时提供可证明的包装编辑鲁棒性。

Abstract: Short-video platforms rank new uploads before anyone has watched them, using content towers that read a video's cover, title and frames. Existing attacks on content-aware recommenders perturb pixels or features and ignore platform re-encoding, and existing defenses train against norm-bounded perturbations that neither match real edits nor preserve recommendation quality. We show with REAP, a realizable attack through {a simulated platform channel (H.264 transcoding, rescaling, cropping and JPEG)}, that packaging edits raise a cold video by 32.0 rank-percentile points in unrelated audiences. We then observe that cold-start ranking does not need the packaging at all: a tower that sees only the video body ranks cold items best. Building on this, we propose Evidence-First Ranking (EFR), which scores cold videos from their body, admits packaging only through a support-gated residual bounded by $γ$, and merges cold and warm rankings by rank percentile, yielding an exact certificate against any packaging edit. On MicroLens-100K, EFR cuts the attack gain by 81% and raises cold-start Recall@100 from 0.1133 to 0.170, while keeping the merged NDCG@10 within 4% of the undefended system.

</details>


### [141] [Prism: Hierarchical Text Detection with Minimalist Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26254)
*Yuchen Su,Xuesheng Wang,Zhilong Ji,Jinfeng Bai,Shijian Lu,Zhineng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出极简层次化文本检测框架Prism：仅用单尺度预训练骨干特征加并行轻量头完成词/行/段多粒度检测，并以几何包含算法重构层次结构，性能SOTA且推理高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有层次化文本检测方法依赖复杂多尺度特征融合颈与重型检测头以保证精度，导致架构臃肿、推理效率低；作者认为充分预训练的骨干可替代这些外部模块，且简单几何包含关系足以解析层次结构。

Method: 将并行轻量检测头附加于单尺度骨干特征图上，分别回归词、文本行、段落三个粒度；通过高效的几何包含算法重建结构层次；并系统比较代表性轻量头范式，确定像素级回归在该设定下精度-效率权衡最佳。

Result: 在多个公开基准上以显著简化的架构取得SOTA：在CTW1500上比LRANet快1.7倍且精度更高；在层次化HierText数据集上PQ指标超过当前SOTA方法OmniParser V2逾6个点。

Conclusion: 验证了"强预训练骨干+轻量并行头+几何包含解析"这一极简范式的有效性，表明层次化文本检测无需复杂多尺度融合与重型头即可兼顾精度与效率。

Abstract: Hierarchical text detection aims to localize words, text lines, and paragraphs while parsing their structural relationships. It provides essential structural cues for visual text understanding. To maximize accuracy, existing methods typically rely on complex multi-scale feature fusion necks and heavy-weight heads, resulting in bloated architectures and low inference efficiency. In this paper, we argue that a sufficiently pre-trained backbone can effectively replace these sophisticated external modules, while simple geometric inclusion suffices for hierarchical text structural parsing. Thus, we propose Prism, a minimalist framework for hierarchical text detection. Prism performs multi-granularity detection by attaching parallel lightweight heads to a single-scale backbone feature map, covering words, text lines, and paragraphs, and reconstructs the structural hierarchy via an efficient geometric inclusion algorithm. In addition, we systematically compare representative lightweight head archetypes and show that pixel-level regression offers the best accuracy-efficiency trade-off in this setting. Experiments on multiple public benchmarks show that our significantly simplified architecture achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance with remarkable efficiency. Specifically, it runs $1.7\times$ faster than LRANet on CTW1500 while achieving higher accuracy, and surpasses the current SOTA method, OmniParser V2, by over 6 PQ points on the hierarchical HierText dataset. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [142] [Digitization of Analogue Gauges using Computer Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26262)
*Erico Kayumba Mutonkole,Olasupo Ajayi,Omowunmi Isafiade*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究开发并评估了一套基于计算机视觉与深度学习的模拟仪表自动读数系统，通过对比三种混合方案（OpenCV+Scikit-Image、OpenCV+ResNet18、OpenCV+VGG19），发现ResNet18以97.3%的准确率显著最优，验证了残差学习在工业仪表数字化中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造业中模拟仪表仍广泛使用，但人工监测易产生人为误差且效率低下，因此需要自动化读数方案以提升精度与效率，并服务于Industry 5.0自动化应用。

Method: 实现并对比三种混合方案：传统计算机视觉（OpenCV+Scikit-Image）、OpenCV+ResNet18、OpenCV+VGG19；通过网页抓取、合成生成与数据增强构建含1,848张压力表图像（0–10 BAR）的数据集。

Result: ResNet18准确率达97.3%，显著优于VGG19的64.4%与Scikit-Image的63.8%。

Conclusion: 残差学习架构在工业仪表数字化任务中效果显著，为Industry 5.0自动化应用提供了可靠基础。

Abstract: Analogue gauges remain prevalent in manufacturing industries for measuring physical quantities, yet manual monitoring by operators introduces human error and operational inefficiency. This research developed and evaluated an automated gauge reading system using computer vision and deep learning techniques. Three hybrid approaches were implemented and compared: OpenCV with Scikit-Image (traditional computer vision), OpenCV with ResNet18, and OpenCV with VGG19 (deep learning approaches). A comprehensive dataset of 1,848 pressure gauge images (0-10 BAR range) was curated through web scraping, synthetic generation, and augmentation techniques. The experimental results demonstrate that ResNet18 significantly outperforms the alternatives, achieving an accuracy of 97.3%, compared to VGG19's 64.4% and Scikit-Image's 63.8%. The study validates the effectiveness of residual learning architectures for industrial gauge digitization, providing a robust foundation for Industry 5.0 automation applications.

</details>


### [143] [Tracking Objects Through Assembly and Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26269)
*Cheryl Lam,Yihong Sun,Bharath Hariharan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出无需初始对象提示的物体状态变化跟踪新范式，构建零样本系统Atlas，通过正向与反向时空划分的联合推理同时恢复物体轨迹并检测分解与组装事件，并发布含密集标注的OmniST基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将传统目标跟踪扩展到状态变化检测的方法依赖初始对象提示，且只能检测该对象的分解，无法处理以多物体为起点的组装事件，因而遗漏了这类变换。

Method: 基于"组装即分解的逆向过程"这一洞见，提出零样本系统Atlas，构建正向与反向时空划分（spatiotemporal partitions）并对其进行联合推理，从而在仅有视频输入的情况下恢复所有物体轨迹并检测分解与组装两类事件；同时构建OmniST基准，提供物体及两类变换的密集标注。

Result: 在OmniST上，Atlas恢复的状态变化数量是先前方法的两倍以上，同时在仅评估分解的现有基准上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 将状态变化跟踪重构为无提示、双向可逆的问题形式，可有效覆盖组装事件，联合正反向时空划分推理是可行的零样本方案，OmniST为完整评估提供了基准。

Abstract: Tracking objects through transformations is essential for understanding real-world dynamics. Recent work has extended traditional object tracking to detect state changes. However, existing methods require an initial object prompt and detect only its decompositions, missing assembly events, which begin with multiple objects rather than one. We therefore introduce a prompt-free formulation of this task, where the goal is to recover all object tracks and detect both decomposition and assembly events given only a video. Our key insight is that assembly is decomposition in reverse. Building on this insight, we present Atlas, a zero-shot system that constructs and jointly reasons over forward and reverse spatiotemporal partitions to recover object tracks and their transformations. We further introduce OmniST, a benchmark with dense annotations of objects and both transformation types. On OmniST, Atlas recovers more than twice as many state changes as prior methods while remaining competitive on existing benchmarks that only evaluate decomposition.

</details>


### [144] [BADAS-3.0: Real-Time Driving Perception and Planning from One Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26294)
*Roni Goldshmidt,Hamish Scott,Lorenzo Niccolini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BADAS-3.0：仅用单目前视相机两秒输入、实时延迟（<50ms）即可预测自车速度、已行驶度量路径、碰撞风险分数与6.4秒内的六条候选未来轨迹，并在多个基准上以远小参数与百倍速度匹配或超越大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究驾驶模型在"单相机+实时延迟"约束下能达到的性能上限，检验现有大参数量、多相机方案的必要性，并弥补现有基准多为常态驾驶、缺乏安全评测的不足。

Method: BADAS-3.0为1B参数的视频编码器加四个小型预测头；输入为单目前视相机两秒视频，输出当前速度、度量路径、碰撞风险分数及6.4秒内六条候选轨迹，推理耗时不足50ms。同时新增两个开环安全基准：真实行车记录仪碰撞/近失场景与Euro NCAP场景。

Result: 给定当前车速，在五个轨迹基准上minADE_6较读六路相机、慢100倍以上的34B Alpamayo 2 Super低44%；该优势来自双方均未训练过的相机配置，在共同配置上两者持平。在两个新增安全基准上，BADAS-3.0的通过率高于所有已测试的已发表规划器。

Conclusion: 单目、小参数、实时延迟的驾驶模型可在大幅降低算力与传感器需求下达到甚至超越超大模型，且跨相机配置泛化是关键增益来源；现有基准存在常态驾驶偏置，需引入安全导向评测。

Abstract: We ask how far a driving model can get with one camera and real-time latency. BADAS-3.0 is a 1B-parameter video encoder with four small heads. From two seconds of one forward camera, it predicts the current speed, the metric path just driven, a collision-risk score and six candidate futures over 6.4 seconds, in under 50 ms. Given the car's current speed, BADAS-3.0 has 44% lower minADE$_6$ over five trajectory benchmarks than the 34B Alpamayo 2 Super, which reads six cameras and runs over 100x slower. The gain comes from camera rigs that neither model was trained on. On the rig they share, the two are on par. Existing benchmarks are mostly nominal driving, so we add two open-loop safety benchmarks, one of real dashcam collisions and near-misses and one of Euro NCAP scenarios. On both, BADAS-3.0 has a higher pass rate than every published planner we tested.

</details>


### [145] [C-LiTE: A Lightweight Foundation Model for Cellular feature learning in Histopathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26316)
*Esha Sadia Nasir,Jiaqi Lv,Gozde N. Gunesli,Shan E Ahmed Raza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: C-LiTE：22M 参数的轻量级细胞级病理基础模型，采用原生分辨率 tokenization 与伪逆权重迁移，在 6000 万细胞裁剪上自监督预训练，7 个数据集冻结特征评测平均 macro-F1 0.670，以约 1/30 参数量逼近 CytoFormer（0.682）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞形态是肿瘤分级、核分裂评估、免疫分型等任务的基础，但多数病理基础模型在组织 patch 上训练，仅隐式编码细胞特征；现有细胞级模型则要么局限于特定细胞群，要么依赖数亿参数的昂贵架构。此外，将 ViT 直接用于小尺寸细胞裁剪存在架构难题：上采样增加算力却不增加形态信息，还可能引入插值伪影。

Method: 提出 C-LiTE（仅 22M 参数）：(1) 面向细胞输入的原生分辨率 tokenization，直接处理 64×64 px 细胞中心裁剪，用 4×4 patch embedding 得到 16×16 token 网格，不做上采样；(2) 通过基于伪逆的变换，将预训练 ViT-S/5 的 patch embedding 权重适配到更细的 patch 分辨率，以迁移大规模预训练视觉知识；(3) 构建 6000 万细胞中心裁剪的预训练数据集（源自 TCGA，均衡覆盖 19 种癌型、16 种组织类型），进行自监督预训练。

Result: 在覆盖核分裂象识别、细胞核与细胞类型分类、免疫细胞表型的 7 个公开数据集上，冻结特征评估取得平均 macro-F1 0.670，接近 CytoFormer 的 0.682，而参数量约少 30 倍。

Conclusion: 原生分辨率 tokenization 加伪逆权重迁移，可在极小参数规模下有效实现细胞级表征学习，说明细胞级病理基础模型不必依赖超大架构或上采样；同时构建了迄今最大的公开 H&E 细胞级自监督预训练数据集，为细胞级计算病理提供高效基线。

Abstract: Characterising individual cells is fundamental to computational pathology, as cellular morphology informs tasks such as tumour grading, mitotic assessment and immune profiling. However, most pathology foundation models are trained on tissue patches and capture cellular features only implicitly, while existing cell-level models are either restricted to specific cell populations or rely on computationally expensive architectures with hundreds of millions of parameters. Applying ViTs directly to small cellular crops also presents an architectural challenge: upsampling increases computational cost without adding morphological information and may introduce interpolation artefacts. To address these limitations, we introduce C-LiTE, a lightweight foundation model for cellular representation learning in H&E-stained histopathology, with only 22 million parameters. C-LiTE employs a native-resolution tokenisation strategy tailored to cellular inputs, processing 64 x 64 px cell-centred crops using a 4 x 4 patch embedding to produce a 16 x 16 token grid without image upsampling. To transfer visual knowledge from large-scale pre-training, we adapt the patch embedding weights of a pre-trained ViT-5-S using a pseudoinverse-based transformation, enabling weight initialisation at the finer patch resolution. We further assemble a pre-training dataset of 60 million cell-centred crops from TCGA, balanced across 19 cancer types and 16 tissue types. To our knowledge, this represents the largest public dataset for self-supervised pre-training on H&E images at the cellular level. We evaluate C-LiTE on seven public datasets spanning mitotic figure recognition, nucleus and cell-type classification, and immune cell phenotyping. Under frozen-feature evaluation, C-LiTE achieves a mean macro-F1 of 0.670, approaching CytoFormer (0.682) with approximately 30x fewer parameters.

</details>


### [146] [ResVision: Spectrum-Matched Resizing and Context-Preserving Inspection for Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26318)
*Guojian Zhan,Jiahao Xu,Yahui Liu,Yao Hu,Chuan Mu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ResVision通过Spectrum-Matched Resizing（自适应全局采样尺度）与Context-Preserving Inspection（裁剪+完整视图）两个互补组件，解决agentic视觉推理中三处瓶颈，在4个前沿VLM、29个基准上全面最优，较Direct Base提升3.36–4.94分、较Agentic Base提升1.87–2.94分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic视觉推理通过选择性放大证据区域而不引入新场景内容来提升VLM，但其收益取决于两个前提：细粒度结构是否仍可获取，局部观察是否仍锚定于完整场景。作者据此归纳出三类反复出现的瓶颈：(1) 高频细节在视觉token形成过程中被衰减；(2) 空间分散的证据虽被编码，但语言模型难以检索；(3) 孤立裁剪因尺度与位置不匹配而削弱“整体—细节”对应关系。

Method: 提出ResVision，含两个互补组件：Spectrum-Matched Resizing（SMR）估计图像复杂度并选择自适应全局采样尺度，对简单输入避免无谓放大、对细节丰富图像分配更多视觉token；Context-Preserving Inspection（CPI）在每次工具调用返回被检视裁剪图的同时附带完整工作视图，从而在跨轮次中同时保留局部细节与全局上下文。实验在Direct与Agentic两种变体下、于4个前沿VLM与覆盖6类视觉推理家族的29个基准上进行评测。

Result: 完整ResVision（Agentic Base + SMR + CPI）在每一个模型上均为最佳配置，相较Direct Base提升3.36–4.94分，相较Agentic Base提升1.87–2.94分。

Conclusion: 结果表明，自适应视觉采样与上下文保持式检视分别针对多模态推理中不同且互补的约束条件，二者结合可稳定带来增益，说明瓶颈定位与组件设计具有对应性和可叠加性。

Abstract: Agentic visual reasoning improves vision-language models by selectively enlarging evidence without adding new scene content, but the benefit depends on whether fine structure remains accessible and whether local observations stay grounded in the full scene. We identify three recurring bottlenecks: high-frequency detail can be attenuated during visual-token formation; spatially distributed evidence can remain encoded yet difficult for the language model to retrieve; and isolated crops can weaken whole-detail correspondence through scale and positional mismatch. ResVision addresses these limitations with two complementary components. Spectrum-Matched Resizing (SMR) estimates image complexity and selects an adaptive global sampling scale, avoiding unnecessary enlargement on simple inputs while allocating more visual tokens to detail-rich images. Context-Preserving Inspection (CPI) returns each inspected crop together with the complete working view, preserving local detail and global context across tool turns. We evaluate Direct and Agentic variants on four frontier VLMs and 29 benchmarks spanning six visual-reasoning families. Complete ResVision (Agentic Base plus SMR plus CPI) is best for every model, improving by 3.36 to 4.94 points over Direct Base and 1.87 to 2.94 points over Agentic Base. These results show that adaptive visual sampling and context-preserving inspection address distinct, complementary constraints in multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [147] [FirstCross: Convex Single-Photon 3D Reconstruction from Sparse Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26321)
*Chi-Wei Lee,Cheng-Han Chiang,Yi-Chun Chang,An-Kai Chen,Po-Chih Kuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FirstCross 是一种无网络、基于体素网格求解短序列凸问题的方法，用于少视角、稀疏光子条件下的单光子激光雷达三维重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经方法（如 Transientangelo）需超过一小时的逐场景非凸优化，且精度随光子通量下降而退化。

Method: 利用"首次穿越"（光线首次进入物体的点）刻画泊松似然：对实心物体，似然仅通过首次穿越点依赖场景。分解为两项——第一项惩罚位于已探测光子前方的表面，具有紧凸闭包（用运行最大值透射率替代 NeRF 的乘积透射率进行体渲染）；第二项处理无表面解释的光子，在假设每个探测表面点后方为厚物体的前提下获得凸上界。整篇方法为在体素网格上求解短序列凸问题。

Result: 在 Transientangelo 自身的真实数据协议下，所有测试场景、视角数与通量水平上深度误差均更低；三视角且光子比协议最低通量还少 100 倍时，平均物体深度误差仍不到 Transientangelo 全光子的一半。最佳经典融合误差为本文三视角误差的 1.3–1.8 倍；真实房间尺度 1% 光子下 TransientNeRF 误差为本文的 2.8–7.7 倍。

Conclusion: FirstCross 使少视角单光子扫描仪可将曝光降至 1%，并在单 GPU 上约一分钟内完成物体重建。

Abstract: Single-photon lidar records the arrival time of each returning photon, enabling 3D reconstruction from few views and scarce photons. However, leading neural methods such as Transientangelo require over an hour of non-convex per-scene optimization, and their accuracy degrades as photon flux decreases. We propose FirstCross, a network-free approach that instead solves a short sequence of convex problems on a voxel grid. For a solid object, the Poisson likelihood of a ray with given signal and background rates depends on the scene solely through its first crossing, the point where the ray first enters the object. Decomposing this likelihood yields two tractable terms. The first penalizes surfaces in front of detected photons and admits a tight convex closure, namely volume rendering with a running-maximum transmittance in place of NeRF's product transmittance. The second accounts for photons that no surface explains and admits a convex upper bound once the object is assumed to be thick behind each detected surface point. Compared with Transientangelo under its own real-data protocol, FirstCross achieves lower depth error across every test scene, view count, and flux level. With three views and 100 times fewer photons than at the protocol's lowest flux, our mean object depth error stays under half of Transientangelo's with all photons. Given our detected surface points, the best classical fusion has 1.3 to 1.8 times our three-view error across flux levels. On real room-scale captures with 1% of their photons, TransientNeRF has 2.8 to 7.7 times our error. FirstCross lets few-view single-photon scanners cut exposure to 1% and reconstruct an object in about a minute on one GPU.

</details>


### [148] [Online Temporal Rotations for 2 Bit KV-Cache Quantization in Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26334)
*Ryan Lucas,Petros Prastakos,Rahul Mazumder*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRQ：为视频扩散Transformer的KV缓存做逐视频自适应的哈达玛-平面旋转量化，2bit下提升VBench一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频生成中KV缓存随去噪步复用迅速成为瓶颈；直接量化时间上相距较远的缓存向量会导致取值范围大、量化误差高，而相邻时间token的缓存存在稳定的逐通道模式，且该模式随视频变化，因此需要随视频自适应的旋转。

Method: 对时间相邻的缓存向量块拟合正交变换，使块内逐通道均值映射到接近各通道常数的向量（mean-flattening目标）；将旋转参数化为仅2比特存储的2×2平面旋转树，后接全局共享的固定Hadamard变换；无需梯度、不改模型权重，证明贪心拟合在mean-flattening目标下全局最优；在线逐视频拟合，并发布融合Triton kernel加速推理。

Result: 2比特、同等压缩率下，VBench subject consistency 由 QVG 的90.38提升至92.02（LongCat-Video-13B），由86.33提升至87.61（HY-WorldPlay-8B），其他指标亦有提升。

Conclusion: 利用KV缓存的时间局部一致性与逐视频差异化分布，轻量在线旋转量化可在极低比特下显著改善长视频生成质量。

Abstract: Video diffusion transformers reuse key--value (KV) caches across denoising steps, which quickly becomes a bottleneck for long video generation. We find that quantizing temporally distant cache vectors leads to a large range and high quantization error, but that nearby video tokens in the KV-cache share a persistent pattern per channel. These patterns also vary across videos, motivating rotations that are adapted to each video's unique cache. We propose Temporal Rotation Quantization (TRQ), a method that rotates frames in the cache that are adjacent in time before quantization. For each block of temporally adjacent cache vectors, TRQ fits an orthogonal transform that maps the block's per-channel means to a vector that is close to a constant across channels. To keep the quantization format lightweight, we parameterize this rotation as a tree of $2 \times 2$ planar rotations, each stored with only two bits, followed by a fixed Hadamard transform shared across all blocks. The rotations are fit without gradients or changes to model weights, and we prove that a simple greedy fit is globally optimal for our 'mean-flattening' objective. The transforms are fit online for each new video, and we release fused Triton kernels to make this process efficient at inference. At 2-bit precision and matched compression, TRQ improves VBench subject consistency over the state-of-the-art method QVG from 90.38 to 92.02 on LongCat-Video-13B and from 86.33 to 87.61 on HY-WorldPlay-8B, as well as improving other metrics.

</details>


### [149] [OffBeat: Multicoset Sampling for 4D Capture Beyond the Camera Frame Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26347)
*Chi-Wei Lee,An-Kai Chen,Cheng-Han Chiang,Yi-Chun Chang,Po-Chih Kuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 OffBeat，将交错触发多相机架建模为空间分布的多陪集采样器，从标定推导同步触发相机组与点火顺序，并给出最小寿命约束与闭式平滑盒核，在 N3DV/PanopticSports 上优于 4DSloMo 与同步采集方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同步多相机架在同一时间戳记录所有视角、丢失帧间运动；4DSloMo 的交错触发虽增加时间戳，但每行相机依次点火使整行等效为一台运动相机，导致深度与运动混淆，且短寿命基元易受同组相机共享的误差（如颜色偏移）影响而产生闪烁。

Method: 将交错相机架视为多陪集采样器（同一采样网格的移位副本）；仅凭标定推导同时点火的相机组及其顺序；证明基元不透明度对时间加权需各组均衡，当且仅当其持续至少一个相机帧间隔；在 Spacetime Gaussians 上用最小寿命近似该界，并以该长度的闭式平滑盒函数精确满足，替代高斯。

Result: 仿真交错采集的 N3DV 上，OffBeat 在全部测试帧上优于含视频扩散的完整 4DSloMo 流程，动态区域平均 PSNR 提升 3.04 dB；同步帧之间优于各场景最佳同步方法（含/不含插帧）1.98 dB；PanopticSports 上与之相当；平滑盒较高斯闪烁更少而 PSNR 接近；替换调度可普遍提升 Spacetime Gaussians、无扩散 4DSloMo 及本文重建的动态区域 PSNR。

Conclusion: 交错触发可等效为多陪集采样，仅改变各相机点火时刻即可在相同帧数下跟踪更快运动，为多相机架 4D 重建提供新的采集与建模范式。

Abstract: For 4D reconstruction, a synchronized multi-camera rig records all views at the same timestamps and misses the motion between them. Staggered triggers spread the same frames over more timestamps. However, when the cameras along each row fire one after another, as in 4DSloMo's schedule on the N3DV rig, the row acts as one moving camera that confounds depth with motion. We introduce OffBeat, which models a staggered rig as a spatially distributed multicoset sampler: shifted copies of one sampling grid. From the calibration alone, OffBeat derives camera groups that fire together and their firing order. Since one group records each timestamp, a short-lived primitive can fit an error its cameras share, such as a color offset, and flicker in novel views. We show that a primitive's opacity over time weights all groups equally, for every number of groups, only if it lasts at least one camera frame interval. On Spacetime Gaussians, a minimum lifetime approximates this bound, and a closed-form smoothed box of that length satisfies it exactly, with less flicker than a Gaussian in its place at nearly equal PSNR. In simulated staggered capture on N3DV, OffBeat outperforms the full 4DSloMo pipeline with video diffusion on every test frame, by 3.04 dB in mean dynamic-region PSNR. Between the first and last synchronized frame, it outperforms the best synchronized method of each scene, with or without frame interpolation, by 1.98 dB in mean dynamic-region PSNR, while synchronized capture is better at its own frames. On PanopticSports, in this frame range, OffBeat is comparable in PSNR to synchronized capture with interpolation. On every N3DV scene, replacing 4DSloMo's schedule with ours raises the dynamic-region PSNR of Spacetime Gaussians, 4DSloMo without diffusion, and our reconstruction. Multi-camera rigs could follow faster motion at the same frame count by changing only when each camera fires.

</details>


### [150] [Contactless Estimation of Respiratory Frequency Using 3D-CNN on Thermal Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26367)
*Federica Gioia,Filippo Pura,Alberto Greco,Dario Piga,Arcangelo Merla,Marco Forgione*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种直接从原始热视频估计呼吸频率的3D-CNN方法，无需复杂预处理与ROI跟踪，在预处理与原始视频上验证集$R^2$均约为0.61。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 呼吸频率等生理参数监测对疾病诊断与管理至关重要，热成像可提供非接触式替代方案，但现有方法依赖繁琐预处理及人工/半自动ROI跟踪，实用性受限。

Method: 构建3D卷积神经网络直接作用于原始热视频数据；针对小数据集问题，采用数据增强与从合成数据集迁移学习进行训练。

Result: 在预处理热视频与原始热视频上验证集$R^2$均约为0.61，证明模型可直接从原始数据估计呼吸频率且性能相当。

Conclusion: 该方法简化了工作流程，提升了热成像在远程医疗、驾驶员监控等实际场景中的可行性。

Abstract: Monitoring physiological parameters such as respiratory rate ($f_R$) is essential for diagnosing and managing various pathological conditions. Thermal imaging offers a promising contactless alternative to traditional methods, which often rely on partially invasive sensors or obtrusive wearable systems. However, existing approaches for $f_R$ estimation from thermal signals typically require extensive pre-processing and manual or semi-automatic region-of-interest (ROI) tracking, limiting their practical applicability. This study proposes a deep learning-based method for estimating $f_R$ directly from thermal videos, eliminating the need for complex pre-processing and ROI tracking. A 3D Convolutional Neural Network (3D-CNN) is developed to operate on raw thermal video data. To address challenges related to small datasets, the model is trained using data augmentation and transfer learning from synthetic datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves a validation $R^2$ score of approximately 0.61 on both pre-processed and raw thermal videos. By simplifying the workflow, this method holds promise for enhancing the feasibility of thermal imaging in real-world applications, such as remote healthcare and driver monitoring in automotive applications.

</details>


### [151] [Fast Self-Supervised Generative CT Denoising via Few-Step Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26370)
*Xingyi Yao,Zhengtao Ma,Zhennong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 N2N-iMF（Noise2Noise with Improved Mean Flow），用少步采样（每样本3次网络评估）加速自监督生成式CT去噪，在避免蒸馏的前提下保持均值平均去噪的可调性，精度与效率均优于DDIM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低剂量与薄层CT噪声大且缺少干净参考；Noise2Noise可自监督但直接回归易过度平滑、丢失细节；多次独立采样平均可缓解模糊，但多步扩散采样代价高。

Method: 基于改进的Mean Flow实现少步生成：N2N-iMF学习以配对噪声图像为条件生成另一幅噪声图像，推理时独立采样多个输出并平均以抑噪；无需蒸馏，平均样本数可在推理时调节。在仿真薄层脑CT、低剂量腹部CT上评测4种采样方法与1–20个平均样本数，并在真实光子计数CT（PCCT）脑扫描上验证。

Result: 每条件输入10样本、每样本3次网络评估时，脑CT MAE 2.60 HU、腹部CT 14.02 HU，优于50步DDIM（2.91、14.53 HU）；同GPU下单输出生成时间由7.29 s降至0.43 s；8例PCCT灰白质CNR为1.341，对比N2N 0.916、DDIM 1.176。

Conclusion: N2N-iMF在无需蒸馏的情况下显著加速自监督生成式CT去噪，并保留推理阶段对平均输出数目的可调性，在仿真与真实数据上同时取得更好的图像质量与采样效率。

Abstract: Low-dose and thin-slice computed tomography (CT) images suffer from elevated noise that obscures anatomical detail, while clean references for supervised denoising are often unavailable. Although Noise2Noise permits self-supervised training from noisy pairs, direct regression tends to oversmooth fine structure. Averaging multiple independently generated samples mitigates this blur, though repeating multi-step diffusion sampling is costly. We introduce Noise2Noise with Improved Mean Flow (N2N-iMF) to accelerate this process via few-step sampling. N2N-iMF learns to generate one noisy image conditioned on its paired counterpart; at inference, independent outputs are sampled and averaged to suppress noise. We evaluated image quality and sampling efficiency on simulated thin-slice brain and low-dose abdominal CT across four sampling methods and averaging counts from 1 to 20, and validated the approach on real-world photon-counting CT (PCCT) brain scans. With ten samples per conditioning input and three network evaluations per sample, N2N-iMF achieved a mean absolute error of 2.60 HU on brain CT and 14.02 HU on abdominal CT, outperforming 50-evaluation denoising diffusion implicit model (DDIM) sampling (2.91 and 14.53 HU). On the same GPU, per-output generation time dropped from 7.29 to 0.43 s. Across eight PCCT cases, N2N-iMF reached a mean gray-white matter contrast-to-noise ratio of 1.341, versus 0.916 for Noise2Noise and 1.176 for DDIM. N2N-iMF accelerates self-supervised generative CT denoising without distillation while keeping the number of averaged outputs adjustable at inference.

</details>


### [152] [EgoCaptioner: Towards Comprehensive and Fine-Grained Egocentric Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26380)
*Yuqi Tang,Yang Shi,Yuran Wang,Tengfei Liu,Rundong Wang,Zongyang Qiu,Qixun Wang,Zhuoran Zhang,Xuanyu Zhu,Yifan Xu,Yiyan Ji,Shujie Li,Shixuan Liu,Haotian Wang,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Chengwei Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EgoCaptioner，用"视频级概览+带时间戳的细粒度步骤"字幕格式、粗到细标注流水线与GRPO多奖励优化，解决第一人称视频字幕中短暂动作漏检、顺序混乱和时序不准问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称（egocentric）视频字幕需要细粒度细节与精确时序，但现有多模态大模型常漏掉短暂动作、混淆动作顺序或误解佩戴者自身运动；同时现有字幕格式难以兼顾"详细叙述"与"精准时间定位"。

Method: 1）设计双层字幕格式：视频级概览（场景、参与者、佩戴者整体活动）+ 自适应细节粒度的带时间戳步骤叙述，并标注关键时刻；2）构建粗到细（coarse-to-fine）标注流水线，先确定事件结构再扩展步骤描述，从八个第一人称视频数据源得到5万条训练样本；3）设计互补奖励（事实准确性、覆盖度、步骤级时间对齐、碎裂/冗余惩罚），用GRPO进行字幕精炼；4）在有传感器数据时引入姿态（pose）推导的运动事实，增强事实监督可靠性。

Result: 在五个第一人称基准上，EgoCaptioner在字幕质量、字幕条件问答与时序对齐方面均取得所评估开源模型中的领先性能；在PhysCapBench上超过Gemini 3.1 Pro，在Minerva-Ego的caption-to-QA协议下超过所有所评估的专有基线。消融实验验证事实奖励与时序奖励的互补作用，以及粗到细标注优于单遍标注。

Conclusion: 细粒度且带时间戳的字幕格式、粗到细标注监督与多目标奖励的强化学习精炼相结合，可有效提升第一人称视频字幕的事实准确性、覆盖度与时序对齐，且开源模型可在此任务上超越部分专有模型。

Abstract: Egocentric video captioning requires fine-grained detail and accurate timing, yet current multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often miss brief actions, confuse their order, or misinterpret the wearer's motion. Existing caption formats also offer limited support for combining detailed narration with precise timing. We present EgoCaptioner, which pairs a video-level overview of the setting, participants, and the wearer's overall activity with timed steps whose narration adapts to varying perceptual demands and marks key moments at specific timestamps. We develop a coarse-to-fine annotation pipeline that establishes event structure before expanding step descriptions, yielding 50,000 training examples from eight egocentric video sources. Complementary rewards assess factual accuracy and coverage, measure step-level temporal alignment, and penalize fragmented or redundant narration, guiding caption refinement through Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). To improve the reliability of factual supervision, we supplement caption references with pose-derived motion facts when sensor data are available. Across five egocentric benchmarks, EgoCaptioner achieves leading performance among evaluated open-source models in caption quality, caption-conditioned question answering, and temporal alignment. It also surpasses Gemini 3.1 Pro on PhysCapBench and all evaluated proprietary baselines on Minerva-Ego under the caption-to-QA protocol. Ablations support the complementary roles of factual and temporal rewards and show that coarse-to-fine annotation provides more effective supervision than single-pass annotation.

</details>


### [153] [SEEC: A Large-Scale Dataset and Benchmark for Self-Supervised External-Eye Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26386)
*George R. Nahass,Michelle Fleishaker,Sanjay Ganesh,Sasha Hubschman,R. V. Paul Chan,Chad A. Purnell,Pete Setabutr,Ann Q. Tran,Sathya N. Ravi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了435万张图像的标准化眼周数据集SEEC及基准，从表示层面与功能层面双重评估自监督眼周表征，发现两者可分离且无单一最优编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部眼部摄影能低成本捕获临床相关的眼周解剖信息，但缺乏大规模标准化资源用于评估自监督外部眼表征。

Method: 从14个异构公开人脸数据集构建435万图像的SEEC语料，并提供留出评估分布、关键点标注解剖探针集及涵盖上睑下垂、甲状腺眼病、颅面综合征的疾病分类实验；在表示层面评估多种数据分布下的嵌入几何，在功能层面评估解剖恢复、OOD解剖迁移与疾病分类；模型包括以InfoNCE、VICReg、LeJEPA预训练的ResNet-101与ViT-B，并与DINOv2、MAE对比。

Result: 表示层面与功能层面评估可分离：更充分的嵌入空间利用并不稳定对应解剖迁移或疾病分类性能；无单一编码器普遍最优。领域特异性外部眼编码器在跨数据集解剖迁移上优于DINOv2与MAE，尽管预训练规模小得多；外部眼预训练ViT在冻结疾病分类探针上匹配或超越通用基线。

Conclusion: 领域专用预训练可为眼周临床任务提供高效表征，且自监督表征评估需兼顾表示与功能两个维度；作者公开基准代码、评估脚本与预处理流程以便复现。

Abstract: External-eye photography captures clinically relevant periocular anatomy at low acquisition cost, yet no large-scale standardized resource exists for evaluating self-supervised external-eye representations. We introduce a 4.35-million-image Standardized External-Eye Corpus (SEEC) derived from fourteen heterogeneous public face datasets, together with a benchmark for external-eye representation learning. For evaluation, we provide held-out evaluation distributions, landmark-annotated anatomical probe sets, and clinical disease-classification experiments spanning ptosis, thyroid eye disease, and craniofacial syndromes. We evaluate representations at two complementary levels: (a) the representation level, by assessing embedding geometry across multiple data distributions, and (b) the functional level, by assessing anatomical recovery, out-of-distribution anatomical transfer, and disease classification. Our models include ResNet-101 and ViT-B encoders pretrained using InfoNCE, VICReg, and LeJEPA objectives and compared with DINOv2 and MAE. Our results indicate that representation-level and functional-level evaluations are dissociable: broader embedding-space utilization does not consistently correspond to anatomical transfer or disease-classification performance, and no single encoder is universally optimal. Domain-specific external-eye encoders outperform DINOv2 and MAE on cross-dataset anatomical transfer despite being pretrained at substantially smaller scale, while external-eye-pretrained ViT models match or outperform these general-purpose baselines on frozen disease-classification probes. We release benchmark code, evaluation scripts, and preprocessing pipelines to reproduce SEEC at https://monkeygobah.github.io/seec-project/

</details>


### [154] [GateTrace: Where Do Crowd Crossings Go Missing Under Occlusion?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26388)
*Ao He,Qianchen Yu,Vivian W. H. Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 GateTrace，用于定位行人跟踪中"漏检穿越"（missed crossings）发生在哪个环节，并评估计数规则修改对召回与精度的影响；在 MOT20 上显示低可见度行人的穿越恢复率（38.9–46.7%）远低于高可见度（98.1–99.0%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型活动中行人跟踪用于出入口与人流通行计数，但总计数掩盖了哪些穿越被漏计。体育场实测显示：清晰可见的穿越约五分之四被记录，而大部分被遮挡的穿越不足三分之一被记录。由于原始视频缺乏逐帧标注轨迹，作者转向带行人轨迹与可见度标注的 MOT20 以系统分析漏检成因。

Method: 提出 GateTrace：1）在固定规则下从标注中导出参考穿越（reference crossings）；2）按人物、方向、时间与位置将系统报告与参考穿越匹配；3）通过保存的检测框与计数决策逐级回溯漏检发生阶段。基于共享检测器比较 ByteTrack、OC-SORT、BoT-SORT 及 MOT20 调优的 BASE，并按可见度分层统计；另做消融——移除 BASE 的后置校验环节，观察漏检与精度变化；并由两名标注员人工复核抽样参考穿越。

Result: 开发序列上，共享检测器下三种跟踪器仅恢复 38.9–46.7% 的低可见度参考穿越，而高可见度为 98.1–99.0%；该差距在另两组序列上按预先固定的协议复现。MOT20 调优 BASE 的公开输出在两组序列的低可见度下恢复 63–78%。不同系统丢失发生在不同阶段：四系统、两组序列中，47–79% 的低可见度漏检属于"无与人物关联的邻近输出"或"保存轨迹中不存在可能穿越"。移除 BASE 的后置校验使多数漏检无法解决，并使相对固定参考列表的精度从约 96% 降至 77%。漏检在经两名标注员看视频确认的抽样参考穿越中依然存在。

Conclusion: GateTrace 能定位穿越在何处丢失，并量化修改后置校验所带来的恢复收益与精度代价；漏检并非单一环节问题，而是跨系统、跨阶段分布。作者将公开代码与逐穿越记录。

Abstract: At large events, pedestrian tracking supports entrance and passageway counts, but totals hide which crossings were missed. In a stadium study, the system recorded about four in five clearly visible crossings but fewer than one in three mostly obscured crossings. The videos lacked trajectories annotated across frames, so we turned to MOT20, which provides pedestrian trajectories and visibility. We introduce GateTrace to locate missed crossings and assess changes to counting rules. It derives reference crossings from annotations under fixed rules, matches reports by person, direction, time and position, and traces misses through saved boxes and counting decisions. With a shared detector, ByteTrack, OC-SORT and BoT-SORT recover 38.9-46.7% of low-visibility reference crossings on development sequences, versus 98.1-99.0% at high visibility. The gap recurs on two further sequences under a protocol fixed in advance. Published outputs of MOT20-tuned BASE recover 63-78% at low visibility across both sequence groups. Losses stop at different stages across systems. Across four systems and both groups, 47-79% of low-visibility misses have no nearby output linked to the person or no possible crossing in the saved tracks. Removing later checks from BASE on development sequences leaves most misses unresolved and lowers precision against the fixed reference list from about 96% to 77%. Misses also remain among sampled reference crossings that two human annotators supported after checking the videos. GateTrace shows where crossings are lost and measures the recovery and precision costs of changing later checks. We will release the code and per-crossing records.

</details>


### [155] [ViDecide: Teaching Video Diffusion Models to Reason and Plan Step by Step](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26389)
*Kaiwen Zhang,Yiming Gao,Zeqi Xiao,Xintao Wang,Pengfei Wan,Kun Gai,Xingang Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以决策为中心的视频扩散训练框架 ViDecide，并配合 Corrective Fine-tuning 校正失败决策，显著提升视频生成模型在多步推理与规划任务中的严格成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型虽能合成高度逼真的视频，但在多步推理与规划中仍频繁违反物理与逻辑约束，即使使用大规模推理数据或现有扩散强化学习方法训练后依然如此。作者将其归因于传统视频训练范式把解视为固定长度片段，而非一系列决策。

Method: ViDecide 将训练建模为决策过程：让视频扩散模型因果地预测状态转移并自主决定何时终止，并采用面向决策的训练调度；进一步提出 Corrective Fine-tuning（CFT），将失败决策对应的噪声映射到模型自身成功的延续轨迹上以纠正错误，无需策略梯度优化即可稳定提升。

Result: 在抽象游戏、规划任务以及新构建的多步机器人规划基准上，ViDecide 平均严格成功率达 83.8%，显著高于标准微调的 23.3% 与最强先前方法的 67.8%，并能泛化到更大规模谜题与未见布局。

Conclusion: 该框架释放了视频生成模型作为视觉世界中可靠推理者与规划者的潜力。

Abstract: Current leading video generation models can synthesize highly realistic videos, while still frequently violating physical and logical constraints in multi-step reasoning and planning, even after training with large-scale reasoning data or existing diffusion RL methods. We attribute these failures to the conventional video training paradigm, which treats a solution as a fixed-length clip rather than a chain of decisions. We present ViDecide, a decision-centric framework that trains video diffusion models to predict state transitions causally and decide when to stop, with a decision-focused training schedule. We further propose Corrective Fine-tuning (CFT), which corrects failed decisions by mapping their noise to the model's own successful continuations, achieving stable improvement without policy-gradient optimization. Across abstract games, planning tasks, and a new benchmark of multi-step robot planning, ViDecide achieves an average strict success rate of 83.8%, far above 23.3% for standard fine-tuning and 67.8% for the strongest prior method, and generalizes to larger puzzles and unseen layouts. Our framework therefore unlocks greater potential for video generation models as reliable reasoners and planners in the visual world. Project page: https://kevin-thu.github.io/ViDecide.

</details>


### [156] [Training Vision Models for LLM-Driven Weight-Space Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26408)
*Timo Ropinski,Sebastian Kranz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文构建了LLM驱动的视觉模型权重空间编程基准，并提出Edit-Ready Training (ERT)训练方法，使网络更易被LLM直接编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明LLM可在权重空间进行编程以直接修改神经网络权重，但专门的网络训练如何影响权重空间编程的成功率尚不清楚。

Method: 在Waterbirds、CIFAR-100、PACS、Entity-30的重标注与纠错任务上，用五个LLM编辑卷积网络与视觉Transformer，共记录2600余次编辑过程，并在留出图像上评估成功率、副作用与精度损失；对比标准训练、标签平滑、基于梯度的可编辑训练与作者提出的ERT（预演LLM所执行的直接闭式权重编辑）。

Result: 为编辑做准备的训练在标准网络难以编辑的情形下显著提升LLM编辑成功率；ERT在全部基线中于GPT-6 Astra与Claude Opus 5.5上取得最高平均分。

Conclusion: 网络的"可编辑性"可由训练塑造，ERT为LLM权重空间编程提供了有效准备；将公开LLM测试框架、任务、编辑轨迹与评分。

Abstract: It has been demonstrated that, in certain settings, large language models can perform weight-space programming to directly modify the weights of neural networks. However, it is unknown how dedicated training of these networks can contribute to the successes of weight-space programming. We present a benchmark of LLM-driven weight-space programming on vision models, in which five LLMs edit convolutional networks and vision transformers on relabeling and error-correction tasks on Waterbirds, CIFAR-100, PACS and Entity-30. We report more than 2,600 such edit processes, whereby each edit is scored on held-out images for success, side effects and accuracy loss. We compare standard training with label smoothing, gradient-based editable training, and our Edit-Ready Training (ERT), a novel method that rehearses the direct, closed-form weight edits LLMs perform. We show that preparing networks for editing raises the success of LLM edits substantially where standard networks are hard to edit. ERT further achieves the highest mean score for GPT-6 Astra and Claude Opus 5.5 across all evaluated baselines. We will release our LLM testing framework alongside all tasks, editing trajectories and scores. Project page: https://viscom.uni-ulm.de/wsp

</details>


### [157] [WeMM-Video: Pushing the Frontiers of Open-Source Omni-Modal Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26409)
*Changli Tang,Jie Yang,Tianyi Wang,Lei Li,Daoze Zhang,Zijie Xin,Zhan Liu,Li Li,Jinchang Xu,Fengyun Rao,Chao Zhang,Jing Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WeMM-Video：中英双语全模态基础模型家族（2B/4B/9B/35B-A3B），以联合音视频感知与推理为核心，采用混合思考范式，在120+基准上达到开源SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音视频联合理解的视频理解仍是多模态智能的核心挑战，现有模型在全模态视频理解与显式推理方面能力不足，且开源社区缺少可复现的中英双语全模态方案。

Method: 构建统一处理文本、图像、音频、视频的全模态模型家族；采用混合思考范式（非思考instruct模式 + 逐步推理thinking模式）；约1.2万亿token大规模语料，渐进式预训练 + 多阶段后训练（multi-teacher OPD、抗重复MrDPO、专家RL）。

Result: 在120+基准上评测：旗舰WeMM-Video-35B-A3B在全模态视频评测中刷新开源SOTA，显著超越Qwen3.5-Omni-Flash、Seed2.0-Mini-0428等专有模型，并缩小与Gemini-3.0-Flash的差距；9B小模型超过更大的开源Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct；在通用音频、无声视频、图像与文本任务上保持高度竞争力。

Conclusion: WeMM-Video 证明了以音视频联合感知与混合思考范式可有效提升全模态视频理解与推理，并以小参数规模实现强性能；公开模型家族及评测/复现流程以促进开源社区研究。

Abstract: Video understanding, particularly with accompanying audio, remains a central challenge in multimodal intelligence. In this report, we introduce \textbf{WeMM-Video}, a family of Chinese-English bilingual omni-modal foundation models spanning 2B, 4B, 9B, and 35B-A3B variants. Centering on joint audio-visual perception and reasoning, WeMM-Video unifies text, image, audio, and video processing within a hybrid-thinking paradigm, seamlessly supporting both a non-thinking instruct mode for fast, direct responses and a thinking mode for explicit step-by-step reasoning. The models are trained on a large-scale multimodal corpus of approximately 1.2 trillion tokens through progressive pre-training and a multi-stage post-training pipeline incorporating multi-teacher OPD, anti-repetition MrDPO, and specialist RL. Evaluated across more than 120 benchmarks, WeMM-Video demonstrates superior performance in omni-modal video understanding and reasoning, while maintaining highly competitive capabilities across general audio, silent video, image, and text tasks. Specifically, in omni-modal video evaluations, our flagship WeMM-Video-35B-A3B establishes a new open-source state of the art, substantially outperforming proprietary models such as Qwen3.5-Omni-Flash and Seed2.0-Mini-0428 while narrowing the gap with the strong Gemini-3.0-Flash, and our compact WeMM-Video-9B surpasses the larger open-source Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct. We publicly release the WeMM-Video family along with our evaluation and reproduction pipeline to foster further research and development in the open-source community.

</details>


### [158] [FFGaussType: Feed-Forward Conversion of Glyph and SVG Geometry into Compact Gaussian Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26431)
*Haoan Feng,Tianfu Wang,Peimeng Guan,Dae Yeol Lee,Guan-ming Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FFGaussType 用单次前向传播将挤出的字形与 SVG 轮廓转换为神经高斯资产，实现可扩展的沉浸式 3D 文本与图标生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沉浸式 3D 场景中的字幕、注释与图标若置于场景内部，可遵循遮挡与透视并接受用户指定的材质与光照。但在高斯场景中，每个新字形或图标都需单独迭代优化，成本高、难以规模化创作。

Method: 提出 FFGaussType：点编码器将已知表面上采样的锚点映射到单一特征平面，再由所有资产共享的解码器预测带表面法线的神经高斯。该设计将几何转换与材质/光照选择解耦，且特征平面支持图像与视频编码以实现紧凑传输。同时构建包含 27,221 个字形与 SVG 图标资产的配对数据集（含锚点及标定后的法线与轮廓渲染），并留出字体系列用于评测。

Result: 准备锚点后的转换耗时 9.1 ms/资产，计算量与内存远低于先前基线；在未见字体族与测试图标上达到 1.62° 法线误差与 0.985 轮廓 IoU，为所比较的前馈转换器中最佳，精度可媲美需数千步逐资产拟合的方法。

Conclusion: 前馈式高斯资产转换可在保证高保真度的同时大幅提升效率与可扩展性，为沉浸式 3D 内容的文本与图标创作提供实用方案；代码与数据集将在论文发表后公开。

Abstract: Immersive 3D content allows text and graphical elements, such as subtitles, annotations, and icons, to be placed inside a scene rather than displayed as flat overlays. Placed this way, they respect occlusion and perspective and take on user-assigned materials and lighting while preserving their original shapes. In Gaussian scenes, however, each new glyph or icon requires its own iterative optimization, which limits scalable content creation. We present FFGaussType, which converts extruded glyph and SVG outlines into Gaussian assets in a single forward pass. A point encoder maps anchors sampled on the known surface to a single feature plane, from which a decoder shared by all assets predicts neural Gaussians carrying surface normals. This separates geometric conversion from material and lighting choices, and the feature plane supports image and video coding for compact delivery. We construct a paired dataset of 27,221 glyph and SVG icon assets with anchors and calibrated normal and silhouette renderings, holding out font families for evaluation. Conversion from prepared anchors takes 9.1 ms per asset, at a fraction of the compute and memory of prior baselines. On unseen font families and test icons, FFGaussType achieves $1.62^\circ$ normal error and $0.985$ silhouette IoU, the best among the compared feed-forward converters, matching the fidelity that per-asset fitting needs thousands of steps to reach. The code and dataset will be released after publication.

</details>


### [159] [AdaPixFlow: Learning Continuous-Time 3D Dynamics Across Scenes from Sparse Multi-view Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26433)
*Hrishikesh Viswanath,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Adaptive Pixel Flows（\ours{}），跨场景学习连续时间动力学算子，将多视角 RGB-D 观测映射为 3D 物体运动学，无需单场景优化或预设物理/运动学假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从视觉观测恢复动态场景中物体的 3D 运动并预测未观测时刻位置是核心问题。现有方法要么针对单个场景优化速度场，要么施加预设的物理与运动学假设，缺乏可跨场景迁移的运动先验。

Method: 将所有相机的观测融合到世界坐标下的共享体素网格中，由网络从该网格预测连续时间速度场，从而学习一个跨场景的连续时间动力学算子；训练仅依赖多视角 RGB-D 观测，无单场景优化与预设运动模型。推理时将速度场从观测状态积分，实现观测间插值与序列外推。

Result: 在留出的 PhysInOne 场景上，方法在中间时刻预测的 F1 比最强对比方法高 2.5%，未来预测高 15%；相比单场景速度场方法分别提升 30% 与 85%；零样本迁移到 Spring-Gaus 上，未来预测 F1 提升 12%。

Conclusion: 跨场景学习连续时间动力学算子是可行且有效的，学到的运动先验可迁移至未见场景，并在插值与外推任务上显著优于逐场景优化与预定义运动模型方法。

Abstract: Understanding how objects move in dynamic scenes requires recovering their 3D motion from visual observations and predicting their positions at unobserved times. Existing methods either optimize scene-specific velocity fields or impose predefined physical and kinematic assumptions. We instead study learning a continuous-time dynamics operator across scenes, mapping multi-view visual observations to 3D object kinematics. We introduce Adaptive Pixel Flows (\ours{}), which learns this operator from multi-view RGB-D observations without per-scene optimization or predefined motion models. The method fuses observations from all cameras into a shared voxel grid in world coordinates, from which the network predicts a continuous-time velocity field. Because the operator is trained across scenes, it learns motion priors that carry over to unseen scenes. The field can then be integrated from observed states to predict object positions between observations and beyond the observed sequence. On held-out PhysInOne scenes, \ours{} improves F1 over the strongest competing method by 2.5\% for in-between prediction and 15\% for future prediction. Compared with per-scene velocity-field methods, the improvements are 30\% and 85\%, respectively. Applied zero-shot to Spring-Gaus, it improves future-prediction F1 by 12\%.

</details>


### [160] [HumanCOIN: A 12,500-Hour Dataset for Human Intent in Egocentric Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26434)
*Shihan Wu,You Liu,Chunyu He,Minghui Qin,Longhan Jiao,Jinbang Guo,Shaoxuan Xie,Kaixi Tian,Wenjie Chen,Jingrui Pang,Yuanhang Zhang,Yichi Zhang,Miao Liu,Tengfei Pan,Yance Jiao,Yunyan Zhang,Yihang Li,Yongjian Shen,Li Liu,Kai Zhu,Chen Fang,Jianxing Chen,Sihan Liu,Xiaofeng Wang,Jie Zhao,Jing Li,Yueqi Wang,Yichen Tang,Wei Li,Zhexuan Yang,Yue Cao,Ji Zhao,Kai Wang,Dongsheng Jiang,Yongwei Wang,Wei Mu,Jiangmiao Pang,Maoqing Yao,Wenchao Ding,Zheng Zhu,Zhongpu Xia,Dalong Du,Jianwei Niu,Dahui Gao,Zhiwei Yu,Bo Lei,Huan Yang,Yonghua Lin,Tiejun Huang,Zhongyuan Wang,Guocai Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 HumanCOIN：以人类意图路径为核心的第一人称操作数据集，12,500 小时、38 类场景，分 L1 注视/注意、L2 表面肌电、L3 头皮脑电三层代理信号，并附多视图 RGB、深度、点云、6-DoF 头腕位姿与步级动作/物体标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有第一人称操作数据集把"观测-动作"对当作原子单元，将动作的生成与执行压缩进策略网络权重，导致动作背后的意图状态不可观测；而人类运动控制研究表明意图在观测与动作之间起中介作用，并在"决策→效应"通路上留下可观测痕迹（注视先于手部到达、肌负荷先于接触建立、皮层活动先于运动指令）。

Method: 构建三层递进式意图代理采集体系：L1 注意层（10,000 h）由头带相机视频推断注视，提前暴露下一注意/抓取目标；L2 用力层（2,000 h）用臂环采集表面肌电，使执行完成前的负荷预期可读；L3 认知层（500 h）采集头皮脑电，提供任务决策与监控的早期读出；每层继承下层全部代理。数据含多视图 RGB、深度图与点云、6-DoF 头/腕位姿、步级动作与物体标注；受控 L3 场景直接测量注视、手部位姿与手指运动学；并随数据集发布时钟与标定数据以支持任意读出率的重新对齐。

Result: 形成 12,500 小时、38 类场景、覆盖三个采集站（从视觉定向到皮层准备）的大规模数据集，L1/L2/L3 分别为 10,000/2,000/500 小时；使"观测-动作"对背后的过程可在数据集尺度上被建模，并提供可复现的时间对齐基础。

Conclusion: 将数据集设计单元从"观测-动作对"上移为"意图通路"，通过注视、肌电、脑电三层代理在规模上记录从决策到效应的过程，为意图感知与建模提供数据基础；局限在于各层代理信号为推断或间接测量、L3 规模较小且限于受控环境。

Abstract: First-person manipulation datasets treat the observation-action pair as the atomic unit and compress the generation and execution of an action into a policy network's weights, rendering the intent state behind the action unobservable. Human motor control research shows that intent mediates between observation and action and leaves observable traces along the intent pathway from decision to effect: gaze reaches the target before the hand, muscle load builds before contact, and cortical activity precedes the motor command. To record this pathway at scale, we present HumanCOIN, a first-person manipulation dataset built around human intent: 12,500 hours across 38 scene classes with three tiers of proxies. At the output end, the L1 attention tier (10,000 h) infers gaze from headband camera video, exposing the operator's next object of attention and reach before the hand moves. Midway, the L2 exertion tier (2,000 h) records surface EMG from armbands, making load expectations readable before execution is complete. At the origin, the L3 cognition tier (500 h) records scalp EEG, giving early readouts of task decisions and monitoring. Each tier inherits all proxies from the tiers below. The dataset further provides multi-view RGB images, depth maps and point clouds, 6-DoF head and wrist pose, and step-level action and object annotations. The controlled L3 setting additionally measures gaze, hand pose and finger kinematics directly. Clock and calibration data are released with the dataset so that any read rate can be re-aligned. The three tiers cover three stations, from visual orienting to cortical preparation, making the process behind visual-action pairs available for modeling at dataset scale. Project page: https://flagopen.github.io/HumanCOIN.

</details>


### [161] [Asuka-FM: A System-Architectural Co-Design Framework for Efficient Text-to-Image Pretraining on Consumer Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26437)
*Víctor Rodrigo González,Pablo M. Olmos,Ignacio Peis,Alejandro Lancho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Asuka-FM 提出一套可在单张消费级 GPU（RTX 4090, 24GB）上从零预训练约 206M 参数双流 DiT 的端到端系统，约 160 GPU 小时、成本低于 55 美元，在 GenEval/DPG 上取得帕累托最优的效率表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿 T2I 基座模型依赖数十亿参数 DiT 与大规模算力集群，学术实验室难以复现。单卡从零训练 DiT 面临三大瓶颈：二次注意力序列开销、变长宽比分桶导致的 JIT 计算图断裂，以及冻结 VAE 主导的单步延迟（吞吐墙）。

Method: 四项核心设计：(i) 无 AdaLN 的双流 DiT 骨干以降低参数量；(ii) Sparse-Dense Residual Fusion (SPRINT) 结构化序列稀疏化，削减注意力 FLOPs 超 50%；(iii) 连续宽高比归一化 RoPE 配合移位方形裁剪训练，保证静态张量形状并实现零样本虚拟相机控制；(iv) 采用速度回归（v-prediction）的轻量 10M 参数 Patch Detailer Head 进行像素适配阶段。

Result: 在无基准特定微调的开放域评测中，256px 下达 GenEval 0.588 / DPG 78.96，512px 下达 GenEval 0.609 / DPG 78.51；训练成本约 160 GPU 小时、低于 55 美元。

Conclusion: 在单张消费级 GPU 上从零预训练紧凑双流 DiT 是可行的，Asuka-FM 为"民主化"基座建模建立了新的帕累托最优效率基线。

Abstract: Frontier text-to-image (T2I) foundation models predominantly rely on multi-billion parameter Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) pretrained across massive compute clusters, creating an accessibility barrier for academic laboratories. When training DiTs from scratch on single consumer GPUs (e.g., an NVIDIA RTX 4090 with 24\,GB VRAM), practitioners encounter three primary bottlenecks: quadratic attention sequence costs, Just-In-Time (JIT) compilation graph breaks induced by variable aspect-ratio bucketing, and an autoencoder throughput wall where frozen Variational Autoencoder (VAE) operations dominate step latency. In this work, we introduce \textbf{Asuka-FM}, an end-to-end system and architectural framework demonstrating that competitive dual-stream DiTs ($\sim$206M parameters) can be pretrained from scratch on a single consumer GPU in $\sim$160 GPU hours for under \$55. Asuka-FM integrates four core designs: (i) an AdaLN-free dual-stream DiT backbone reducing parameter count; (ii) structured sequence sparsification via Sparse-Dense Residual Fusion (SPRINT) to cut attention FLOPs by over 50\%; (iii) continuous aspect-normalized Rotary Position Embeddings (RoPE) coupled with shifted square crop training, guaranteeing static tensor shapes while unlocking zero-shot virtual camera control; and (iv) a pixel adaptation phase utilizing a lightweight (10M parameter) Patch Detailer Head operating under velocity regression ($v$-prediction). Evaluated on open-domain benchmarks without benchmark-specific fine-tuning, Asuka-FM achieves GenEval 0.588 / DPG 78.96 scores at 256px and GenEval 0.609 / DPG 78.51 scores at 512px, setting a new Pareto-optimal efficiency standard for democratic foundation modeling.

</details>


### [162] [Risk-Controlled Test-Time Adaptation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26438)
*Zixuan Liu,Hashem Haghbayan,Zizhan Zheng,Siavash Khajavi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 R2-VLA：风险可控的检索增强 VLA 框架，通过学习不确定性预测器判断何时自主执行、何时请求专家介入，并以任务级风险控制标定阈值，在满足用户指定成功率要求下最小化专家辅助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强 VLA 虽可在测试时利用专家演示做免参数更新的适配，但即便检索到的演示与当前任务相关，模型仍可能无法有效利用这些信息而导致任务失败。现有方法缺乏对"检索信息是否真被有效利用"的判断机制，也无法保证任务级成功率的可靠性。

Method: 1) 学习不确定性预测器：以检索到的演示为条件，估计 VLA 预测动作的不确定性，据此决定自主执行还是请求专家协助；2) 任务级风险控制流程：标定不确定性阈值，使用户指定的任务失败风险（成功率）要求得到满足；3) 持续将专家纠正写入检索记忆以改进后续适配，同时保持既定任务级风险保证。

Result: 在 3 个 VLA 主干网络与 4 个 LIBERO 基准套件上，平均任务成功率提升 9.2–11.2 个百分点，且以更少的专家请求达到目标成功率。

Conclusion: 将检索演示的"有用性"判定与任务级风险控制相连接，可为机器人操作提供可靠、可验证的测试时适配，在成功率与专家开销之间取得更优权衡。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented vision-language-action (VLA) models enable efficient test-time adaptation by leveraging expert demonstrations as additional context without updating model parameters. However, even when retrieved demonstrations are relevant to the current task, the VLA may fail to effectively utilize the retrieved information, ultimately leading to task failure. To address this challenge, we propose R2-VLA, a risk-controlled retrieval-augmented VLA framework that enables reliable test-time adaptation by satisfying a user-specified task success rate requirement with minimal expert assistance. Specifically, R2-VLA learns an uncertainty predictor that estimates the uncertainty of the VLA's predicted actions given the retrieved demonstrations, guiding when to act autonomously or request expert assistance. We further introduce a task-level risk-control procedure that calibrates the uncertainty threshold to satisfy a user-specified task-failure risk requirement. Moreover, R2-VLA continually incorporates expert corrections into its retrieval memory to improve future adaptation while preserving the prescribed task-level risk guarantee. Experiments across three VLA backbones and four LIBERO benchmark suites demonstrate that R2-VLA improves average task success by 9.2--11.2 percentage points, achieving the desired target with fewer expert requests. These results highlight the effectiveness of connecting the usefulness of retrieved demonstrations with task-level risk control to enable successful robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [163] [Fixing the Last Mile: Failure-Aware Image Restoration with Region-Specific Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26456)
*Hongyu An,Fanghua Yu,Kainan Yan,Luc Van Gool,Chao Dong,Jinjin Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Failure-Aware Image Restoration (FAIR)，通过区域级条件化（预测故障易发区域并给出对应修复指引）实现单次推理的可控局部精修，解决图像复原的"最后一公里"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有复原方法已显著提升全局保真度，但高质量结果仍在语义关键区域（人脸、文字）出现伪影、结构不一致与纹理错配；这些局部错误虽占比小，却主导整体感知质量，即"最后一公里"问题。

Method: 不再对整图施加统一的生成先验，而是将单次前向的复原模型条件化于两类信息：(1) 局部故障可能出现的位置；(2) 各区域所需的纠正性引导。以此实现区域绑定的条件化（region-bound conditioning），进行细粒度、可控的复原。作为现有复原流程的补充模块，将重点从全局保真转向局部感知可接受性。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法能解决常见故障案例，在提升关键区域语义准确性与风格一致性的同时保持其他区域的高质量忠实复原，整体感知质量优于基线。

Conclusion: FAIR以局部故障感知与区域条件化为核心，弥补技术指标与实际可用性之间的差距，为图像复原提供从全局保真到局部感知可接受性的范式补充。

Abstract: Recent advances in image restoration have greatly improved global image fidelity, producing visually plausible results across most regions of an image. Nevertheless, even high-quality restorations often exhibit localized failures, including artifacts in semantically important regions such as faces and text, structural inconsistencies, and texture mismatches. Although these errors typically affect only a small portion of the image, they often dominate the overall perceptual quality of the result. In this work, we propose Failure-Aware Image Restoration (FAIR) to address the "last-mile" problem in image restoration. Rather than applying one uniform generative prior over the whole image, we condition a single-pass restoration model on both where local failures are likely to occur and what corrective guidance each region requires. This region-bound conditioning enables fine-grained and controllable restoration that improves semantic accuracy and stylistic consistency in critical regions while maintaining faithful, high-quality restoration elsewhere. Our method complements existing restoration pipelines by shifting the emphasis from global fidelity to local perceptual acceptability. Extensive experiments show that the proposed approach resolves common failure cases and improves perceptual quality, bridging the gap between technical accuracy and practical usability.

</details>


### [164] [SHUSH: Emission-Aware Reward Design for Proactive Video Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26478)
*Shawn Liang,Soamar Homsi,Gourav Datta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有主动视频助手的强化学习奖励（PAUC）不惩罚发言数量，导致过度应答；本文提出考虑发言次数的F1奖励，在OmniPro与ProactiveVideoQA上显著减少冗余回答并提升时序与内容F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动视频助手不仅需决定"说什么"，还需决定"何时说"。现有RL目标（如MMDuet2的PAUC）只奖励及时且正确的响应，不计入响应数量，从而系统性地鼓励多余或重复的发言。

Method: 解析证明PAUC对额外响应仅施以弱惩罚甚至零惩罚，导致过度发言。进而针对区间型标注提出emission-aware F1奖励，通过独占的span–emission匹配机制对错误发言与漏检区间计价，并在MMDuet2的训练配方下进行策略训练。

Result: 在OmniPro视觉子集上，回复频率降低2.5倍，时序F1从0.259升至0.396，内容感知F1从0.103升至0.154；保守变体减少82.5%回复，精确率从0.161升至0.418。ProactiveVideoQA上该奖励在基线近乎沉默处补足回复；而PAUC将严重过度发言的策略排至第二，尽管其F1接近最低。

Conclusion: 主动助手应以对发言数量计价的奖励进行训练与评估，以抑制冗余应答并真实反映策略质量。

Abstract: Proactive video assistants must decide not only what to say, but also when to speak. Existing reinforcement-learning objectives can reward timely, correct responses without accounting for response volume, thereby encouraging unnecessary or repeated emissions. We identify this failure mode in MMDuet2, whose proactive area-under-the-curve (PAUC) reward promotes timely responses but does not price the number of emissions. We show analytically that this makes additional responses weakly penalized or effectively free, systematically encouraging over-emission. We then propose an emission-aware F1 reward for interval-valued annotations, using exclusive span--emission matching to price false emissions and missed spans. Under MMDuet2's training recipe, our policy on the visual subset of OmniPro replies 2.5x less often than MMDuet2 while raising timing F1 from 0.259 to 0.396 and content-aware F1 from 0.103 to 0.154; a more conservative variant cuts replies by 82.5% and raises precision from 0.161 to 0.418. On ProactiveVideoQA, the same reward adds replies where the baseline is nearly silent, and PAUC ranks a heavily over-emitting policy second despite near-last F1, because it is insensitive to response count. Our results indicate that proactive assistants should be trained and evaluated with objectives that price emissions.

</details>


### [165] [SGAR: Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction via Sparse Geometry-Guided Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26493)
*Aoxiang Fan,Maria Pilligua,Corentin Dumery,Oliver Heinimann,Pascal Fua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SGAR用稀疏几何注意力替换前馈三维重建中的稠密全局注意力，由学习到的键选择模块预测跨视角三维邻近关系，从而将复杂度从随视角数平方增长降为稀疏高效，在速度与精度上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈重建模型采用稠密全局注意力关联所有视角的patch，计算代价随视角数呈二次增长，难以扩展到大规模重建场景。作者从经典SfM视角指出：三维结构源于二维区域间的几何匹配，因此大部分稠密注意力计算是冗余的。

Method: 提出SGAR（Sparse Geometric Attention Reconstruction）。每个patch token仅与其他视角中少量patch token进行注意力交互，这些token由轻量级可学习键选择模块挑选，该模块被训练用于预测token间的跨视角三维邻近性；以几何相似性引导的稀疏注意力替代稠密全局注意力。SGAR为通用方法，可通过微调应用于不同的前馈模型。

Result: 在实验中，SGAR是现有稠密与稀疏SOTA方法中最快的，也是稀疏方法中最准确的；在1000个视角下比稠密注意力快最多12倍，且在现有稀疏方法失效时仍保持稳定的位姿精度；在相同时间预算下重建出更完整的几何。

Conclusion: 以几何相似性为引导的稀疏注意力是稠密全局注意力的高效且有效的替代方案，SGAR可作为通用插件提升前馈三维重建的可扩展性。

Abstract: Feed-forward models have been very successful in 3D reconstruction with ad hoc attention designs that relate all view patches densely. However, the cost of this dense global attention grows quadratically with the number of views, preventing the model from applying to larger-scale reconstruction settings. In this paper, we argue that most dense attention computations are unnecessary from a classical structure-from-motion perspective: 3D structures stem from geometrically matching regions in 2D. We bring this principle to learning-based feed-forward reconstruction with our Sparse Geometric Attention Reconstruction (SGAR) method. In SGAR, each patch token attends to only a small set of patch tokens from other views, chosen by a lightweight learned key selection module trained to predict cross-view 3D proximity of tokens. Thus, dense global attention is replaced by much more efficient geometric-similarity-guided sparse attention. SGAR is a general method that can be applied to different feed-forward models by fine-tuning. In our experiments, we show that SGAR is the fastest among both state-of-the-art dense and sparse methods and the most accurate sparse one. It runs up to 12-times faster than dense attention at 1,000 views with stable pose accuracy where existing sparse methods fail, and shows the best performance in reconstructing more complete geometries on the same time budget.

</details>


### [166] [Direct3D-V3: Latent-Free Diffusion Transformers in the Native Voxel Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26511)
*Shuang Wu,Jiachen Qian,Youtian Lin,Feihu Zhang,Philip Torr,Yao Yao,Xun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Direct3D-V3，一种无潜空间（latent-free）的扩散 Transformer 框架，抛弃 VAE，直接在原生 SDF 体素域建模 3D 几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有原生 3D 生成普遍依赖潜空间扩散，聚焦于更强的 VAE 与更复杂的潜表示（vecset、triplane、结构化稀疏体素），但都存在"先压缩再生成"的重建瓶颈，限制了局部细节。

Method: 1) DiT 直接在原始 SDF 体素网格上训练与采样，不设 VAE；2) 对相邻体素施加隐式流形正则，抑制非水密表面；3) 用 Marching Cubes 将预测 SDF 转为网格，并以渲染法线损失直接监督；4) 辅助预测条件图像的相机参数，强化图像到几何的映射。

Result: 消除重建瓶颈，目标函数直接定义在原生体素域，生成网格具有显著更丰富的局部细节。

Conclusion: 预测的相机与原生 SDF 体素输出使框架可扩展至生成式重建：扩散与法线引导细化交替进行，提升与观测视图的对齐，并由生成先验补全未见区域。

Abstract: Native 3D generation has largely been built around latent diffusion, with recent advances concentrating on stronger variational autoencoders (VAE) and increasingly elaborate latent representations, from vecset and triplanes to structured sparse voxels. Despite their architectural differences, these methods all share the same premise: geometry is first compressed into a latent space and only then modeled by the generator. We take a different route and propose Direct3D-V3, a latent-free diffusion transformer (DiT) framework that discards the VAE altogether and operates in the native voxel domain, directly modeling raw SDF voxel grids during both training and sampling. To make this direct voxel formulation geometrically reliable, we introduce an implicit manifold regularization over neighboring voxels to suppress non-watertight surfaces, and convert predicted SDF grids into meshes via Marching Cubes so that rendered normal losses can supervise the model directly from its predicted SDF. To improve alignment between the conditioning image and generated geometry, Direct3D-V3 also predicts the camera parameters of the conditioning image as an auxiliary task, encouraging the DiT to learn the mapping from image observations to 3D shape. This latent-free design removes the reconstruction bottleneck, brings training under an objective in the native voxel domain, and yields meshes with substantially richer local detail. The predicted camera and native SDF voxel output further make Direct3D-V3 extensible to generative reconstruction, where diffusion is interleaved with normal-guided refinement to improve alignment with the observed view while the generative prior completes unseen regions. Project page: https://dreamtechai.github.io/Direct3D-V3.

</details>


### [167] [Plan4D: Generative Plannable 4D Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26516)
*Hangwei Zhang,Rujing Dang,Ruimin Lin,Jiahong Wu,Xiangxiang Chu,Chuanxia Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: plan4d 提出一种生成式可规划4D世界模型，从场景上下文与文本动作预测未来RGB视频及稠密4D几何（深度、相机、点云、场景流），通过模型缝合架构实现SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型偏重生成视频的美学质量，缺乏显式4D建模，导致物理基础薄弱、时序几何一致性差；而配对"动作+稠密4D标注"的训练数据天然稀缺。

Method: 采用模型缝合（model-stitching）架构，将 Depth Anything 3（3D重建先验）与 LongCat-Video（视频生成先验）耦合并互补，并附加辅助 DPT 头用于场景流预测，从而在动态3D场景中规划并生成长程、细粒度动作的未来。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上的大量实验表明，plan4d 在环境反应的生成式4D建模上达到SOTA，同时显著提升生成未来视频的视觉保真度与几何精度。

Conclusion: plan4d 被视为迈向可规划4D生成世界模型的潜在下一里程碑，验证了借助基础视频生成器与3D重建器互补先验、绕开数据稀缺瓶颈的可行性。

Abstract: Most world models for future prediction prioritize aestetic quality of generated videos, but offer only weak physical grounding and limited geometric consistency over time, due to the lack of explicit 4D modeling. In this paper, we introduce plan4d, a possible next milestone towards building a generative plannable 4D world model that predicts future RGB videos together with dense 4D geometry, including depth, cameras, point clouds, and scene flow from an observed scene context and text-specified actions. Since training data with paired actions and dense 4D annotations is inherently scarce, plan4d leverages the complementary priors of foundational video generators and 3D reconstructors. Specifically, we propose a model-stitching architecture that couples Depth Anything 3 with LongCat-Video and augments it with an auxiliary DPT head for scene-flow prediction. The resulting model allows us to plan and generate complex, long-horizon, and fine-grained actions in a dynamic 3D scenes, while substantially improving both visual fidelity and geometric accuracy of the generated future videos. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that plan4d achieves state-of-the-art performance in generative 4D modeling of environmental reactions. Project Page: https://fogradio.github.io/plan4d-project

</details>


### [168] [Deterministic Temporal Embedding for Calcium Imaging Trace Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26522)
*Qi Gao,Julius Beichert,Miriam Ratliff,Karl Rohr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出确定性的时间嵌入框架，将像素级荧光迹映射到低维表示，使时间相干的源形成空间相干区域，从而无形状先验地提取钙成像迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钙成像迹的可靠提取依赖于识别荧光反映同一细胞活动的像素，但现有方法常依赖显式细胞形状先验或随机负采样，导致对不规则、空间相接的源分割不稳定。

Method: 以模糊邻域图与谱初始化出发，在低维嵌入上优化类平衡交叉熵目标；稀疏吸引项精确计算，致密排斥项采用确定性粒子-网格分解并配合稀疏边补偿，从而避免随机负采样；最后将潜表示空间解码为源支撑以提取迹。

Result: 在合成数据与活体双光子钙成像数据上，稳健恢复了不规则及空间相接的源，并实现了高效的确定性嵌入优化。

Conclusion: 该确定性嵌入框架无需细胞形状先验即可获得可靠源支撑，兼具稳健性与计算效率，适用于钙成像迹提取。

Abstract: Reliable extraction of calcium-dependent fluorescence traces in calcium imaging sequences requires identifying pixels whose fluorescence intensity reflects the same underlying cellular activity. We present a deterministic temporal embedding framework that maps pixel-wise fluorescence traces to a low-dimensional representation in which temporally coherent sources form spatially coherent regions. Starting from a fuzzy-neighborhood graph and spectral initialization, we optimize a class-balanced cross-entropy objective using exact sparse attraction and a deterministic particle-mesh decomposition of the dense repulsive interaction with sparse edge compensation, avoiding stochastic negative sampling. The resulting latent representation is spatially decoded into reliable source supports for trace extraction without imposing an explicit cellular shape prior. Experiments on synthetic and in vivo two-photon calcium imaging data demonstrate robust recovery of irregular and spatially touching sources together with efficient deterministic embedding optimization.

</details>


### [169] [AURA: AUdio-dRiven streaming Avatar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26540)
*Nathaniel Cohen,Nicolas Dufour,Amélie Royer,Alasdair Newson,Patrick Pérez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AURA 是一个流式音频驱动说话头视频扩散模型，采用滚动KV缓存逐块生成，配合两阶段蒸馏（轨迹对齐 + 带反向模拟的DMD），在单卡上实现1.3B模型576p实时（20.7 fps）、5B模型720p达9.7 fps，速度较双向教师模型快17–23倍且质量相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频驱动说话头视频扩散模型需对多秒视频块执行数十步去噪，延迟高、非因果，难以满足语音助手、虚拟形象等流式应用对低延迟与在线因果生成的需求；同时双向模型需多秒音频前瞻，不适合实时场景。

Method: 提出AURA，逐块生成视频并维护滚动KV缓存，仅需当前块的音频即可开始生成；提供1.3B与5B两种规模。两阶段蒸馏流水线：第一阶段通过更少扩散步数与块级滑动窗口因果注意力掩码，使因果学生模型匹配教师模型的轨迹以完成对齐；第二阶段采用带反向模拟的Distribution Matching Distillation（DMD）弥合剩余质量差距。

Result: AURA 1.3B在单GPU上以20.7 fps流式输出576p（对应16 Hz播放率实时），5B模型以9.7 fps输出720p；在标准说话头基准上，1.3B比其双向教师快23倍且低分辨率下质量与TalkVerse相当，5B在720p下质量匹配TalkVerse教师且快17倍。同时开源两模型权重、30K合成视频数据集及预计算教师轨迹。

Conclusion: AURA证明通过因果逐块生成与两阶段蒸馏可在保持教师级质量的同时实现实时流式说话头合成，为流式虚拟形象生成提供了可用方案，并通过开源权重与数据集支持复现与后续研究。

Abstract: Current audio-driven video diffusion models can synthesize realistic talking-head videos but generally require dozens of diffusion denoising steps over multi-second video chunks, making them unsuitable for streaming applications such as voice assistants and virtual avatars, where low-latency and online causal generation are required properties. AURA generates video chunk by chunk with a rolling KV cache, requiring only the audio of the current chunk to start generating, rather than a multi-second audio look-ahead like bidirectional models. It comes in two sizes: AURA 1.3B streams 576p video in real time on a single GPU (20.7 fps for a 16 Hz playback rate), making it suited to interactive avatar applications, and AURA 5B streams 720p video at 9.7 fps. Our two-stage distillation pipeline first aligns the causal student with the teacher, by training it to match the teacher's trajectories with fewer diffusion steps and a chunk-wise sliding window causal attention mask, then applies Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) with backward simulation to close the remaining quality gap. On standard talking-head benchmarks, AURA 1.3B runs 23x faster than its own bidirectional teacher and reaches a quality comparable to TalkVerse at a lower resolution, and AURA 5B matches its TalkVerse teacher at 720p while running 17x faster. To support reproducibility and further research on streaming avatar generation, we release the weights of both models along with a dataset of 30K synthetic videos and their precomputed teacher trajectories.

</details>


### [170] [Multi-institutional grading of Barrett's esophagus dysplasia without stain normalization using a pathology foundation model and multiple instance learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26562)
*Amirali Khosravi,Jaidip M. Jagtap,D. Chamil Codipilly,Catherine E. Hagen,Jason T. Lewis,Rickey E. Carter,Shahriar Faghani,Vani J. A. Konda,Fouad Otaki,Shajan Peter,Nicholas Shaheen,Sachin Wani,Prasad G. Iyer,Bradley J. Erickson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 基于基础模型的深度学习系统用于Barrett食管异型增生分级，在多机构969张WSI上实现高准确度（测试集AUC 0.96）且跨机构一致、无需染色归一化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Barrett食管异型增生的组织学分级（尤其低级别异型增生）存在显著的观察者间差异，影响临床决策，需要客观、可复现的辅助诊断工具；同时既往系统依赖机构特异的染色归一化预处理，限制了跨中心部署。

Method: 回顾性、多机构诊断准确性研究。数据为4家机构的969张H&E全切片图像。先用专家标注训练块级分类器定位诊断相关区域，再采用属性感知的多示例学习（attribute-aware MIL）进行切片级三分类：非异型增生Barrett食管（NDBE）、低级别异型增生、高级别异型增生。在130例独立测试集上评估，未做染色归一化。

Result: 测试集宏平均AUC 0.96（95% CI 0.93–0.98）；二分类异型增生检测敏感度98%（80/82）、特异度81%（39/48），阴性似然比0.030，与专家共识的二次加权kappa为0.83；无高级别异型增生被误判为NDBE；各机构间性能一致。

Conclusion: 基础模型表征具有良好的染色鲁棒性，可省去既往系统所依赖的机构特异预处理，从而支持跨机构、多中心的部署应用。

Abstract: Barrett's esophagus dysplasia grading suffers substantial inter-observer variability, particularly for low-grade dysplasia, complicating clinical decision-making. We conducted a retrospective multi-institutional diagnostic accuracy study of a foundation model-based deep learning system for dysplasia grading, using 969 H\&E-stained whole slide images from four institutions. Patch-level classifiers trained on expert annotations identified diagnostically relevant regions, followed by attribute-aware multiple instance learning for slide-level classification as non-dysplastic Barrett's esophagus (NDBE), low-grade dysplasia, or high-grade dysplasia. On a held-out test set (n = 130), macro-averaged AUC was 0.96 (95\% CI 0.93--0.98). For binary dysplasia detection, sensitivity was 98\% (80/82) and specificity 81\% (39/48), with a negative likelihood ratio of 0.030 and quadratic-weighted \kap{} of 0.83 against expert consensus. No high-grade dysplasia case was misclassified as NDBE. Performance was consistent across institutions without stain normalization, indicating that stain-robust foundation-model representations may remove the site-specific preprocessing that has constrained prior systems to institution-specific deployment.

</details>


### [171] [Coda-DaVinci: Reimagining Video Generation with Code2Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26595)
*Yiyan Ji,Qixun Wang,Yang Shi,Zhuoran Zhang,Yifan Xu,Tengfei Liu,Yuqi Tang,Xiaohan Zhang,Shujie Li,Jiafu Tang,Xuanyu Zhu,Xinyu Liu,Yan He,Yushuo Guan,Zihao Wang,Xianghua Ying,Shixuan Liu,Haotian Wang,Wenjing Yang,Zhaoxiang Zhang,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Jiaheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了Code2Video中代理（3D proxy）的粒度、物理仿真与提示设计对视频生成质量的影响，并提出任务自适应的多智能体框架Coda-DaVinci。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Code2Video利用代理构建的3D代理指导视频生成，可显式控制结构与动态，但如何构建代理与提示以获得最佳下游质量尚不明确，该范式的能力与局限也缺乏研究。

Method: 通过受控实验与盲评人评，考察代理粒度、物理仿真与提示策略；基于发现提出Coda-DaVinci：在代理引导与直接生成间自适应选择，按实体调整建模粒度，协调仿真与基于代理的提示，并采用多智能体框架保存场景资产、通过视觉检查与独立评审引导修复。

Result: 粗粒度代理利于自然运动，中粒度代理利于一致性；物理仿真与代理对齐提示可提升平均质量。Coda-DaVinci在VTR指令遵循、R2M场景一致性上领先，物理合理性优于直接生成，视觉真实感优于Code2Video基线；长视频在事件结构与连续交互一致性上有提升，但转场稳定性与场景设定遵循仍存局限。

Conclusion: 代理引导的Code2Video存在任务相关的权衡，需自适应选择粒度与生成路径；Coda-DaVinci验证了该思路的有效性，同时揭示转场稳定性与场景遵循为未来改进方向。

Abstract: Code2Video uses agent-authored 3D proxies to guide video generation, enabling explicit control over structure and dynamics. However, how to construct proxies and prompts for optimal downstream quality remains underexplored, as do the capabilities and limitations of this paradigm. Through controlled experiments and blinded human evaluation, we study proxy granularity, physics simulation, and prompting. The results reveal task-dependent trade-offs: coarse proxies favor natural motion, while medium-grained proxies favor consistency. Physics simulation and proxy-aligned prompting further improve average quality. Guided by these findings, we introduce Coda-DaVinci, a task-adaptive agentic framework for Code2Video generation that selects between proxy-guided and direct generation, adapts modeling granularity to each entity, and coordinates simulation with proxy-grounded prompting. Its multi-agent harness preserves scene assets and guides repairs through visual inspection and independent review. Experiments demonstrate leading instruction following on VTR and scene consistency on R2M, improved physical plausibility over direct generation, and higher visual realism than the compared Code2Video baselines. Long-video evaluations further show gains in event structure and consistency across successive interactions, while revealing limitations in transition stability and adherence to scene settings.

</details>


### [172] [Depth Pro 2: Sharp and Temporally Consistent Metric Monocular Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26603)
*Peiyun Hu,Marko Mihajlovic,Lyndon Duong,Lahav Lipson,Wei Dong,Amaël Delaunoy,Stephan R. Richter,Vladlen Koltun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Depth Pro 2 提出一个统一的、可扩展的网络，同时处理图像与视频，在原生分辨率下预测稠密的度量深度，兼具度量精度、边界锐利与时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目深度估计方法存在取舍：多帧方法偏向时间一致性，单帧方法偏向边界锐利度；作者希望打破这一权衡，同时获得一致性、锐利度与度量精度。

Method: 分析先前方法后，训练单一可扩展网络，统一处理图像和视频输入，并在原生分辨率上直接预测深度图。

Result: 输出的深度图同时具备度量准确、边界锐利和时间一致，在上述各维度均优于先前工作；并发布了一组在度量精度、锐利度、时间一致性和运行时上构成新 Pareto 前沿的模型系列。

Conclusion: 通过统一训练框架可同时兼顾时间一致性与边界锐利度并保持度量精度，模型族在多项指标上定义新的 Pareto 前沿，代码与权重已开源。

Abstract: We present Depth Pro 2, an approach to estimating dense metric depth for images and video at full resolution. By analyzing prior methods for monocular depth estimation, we find that they commonly trade off temporal consistency and boundary sharpness: multi-frame methods favor consistency, single-frame methods favor sharpness. To overcome this trade-off, we train a single, scalable network that processes both images and video and predicts depth maps at native resolution. Depth Pro 2 produces depth maps that are metrically accurate, sharp, and temporally consistent all at once, outperforming prior work in all of these dimensions. We release a family of models that define a new Pareto frontier in terms of metric accuracy, sharpness, temporal consistency, and runtime. We release code and weights at https://apple-aiml-research.github.io/ml-depth-pro-2/

</details>


### [173] [ScaleWorld: High-Resolution World Generation through Shared Prediction and Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26615)
*Yansong Qu,Wesker Zheng,Quanjian Song,Zhiyuan Zhang,Xiao Cao,Shengchuan Zhang,Liujuan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScaleWorld 是一个因果视频世界模型，在单一共享主干内统一低分辨率世界预测与高分辨率精炼，通过精炼自身预测实现高效流式高分辨率视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率生成可呈现精细场景结构与纹理，但直接生成高分辨率视频的计算与显存开销巨大；而使用独立精炼模型又会增加系统复杂度与成本。

Method: 共享因果主干对每个视频块先做低分辨率预测，再以该预测为条件执行一步高分辨率精炼；分别维护长程低分辨率历史（预测）与短程高分辨率历史（精炼）两套因果状态以支持流式生成；针对蒸馏中的任务干扰，将专家优化与能力整合分离，提出任务非对称在线策略蒸馏（TA-OPD），在各任务的采样与历史策略下通过局部速度匹配将预测与精炼行为迁移至共享学生模型。

Result: 大量实验表明，ScaleWorld 在实现高质量世界生成的同时提升了计算与显存效率。

Conclusion: 将低分辨率预测与高分辨率精炼耦合于共享主干，并配合非对称在线蒸馏，可在无需独立精炼模型的前提下兼顾生成质量与效率，为高分辨率视频世界模型提供可行方案。

Abstract: High-resolution generation enables video world models to render fine-grained scene structures and textures, but directly generating high-resolution video incurs substantial computational and memory costs. We present ScaleWorld, a causal video model that unifies low-resolution world prediction and high-resolution refinement within a single shared backbone. By refining its own predictions, ScaleWorld produces high-resolution world videos without requiring a separate refinement model. For each video chunk, the shared backbone first completes a low-resolution prediction and then performs one-step high-resolution refinement conditioned on that prediction. To support efficient streaming generation, we maintain separate causal states with a long low-resolution history for prediction and a short high-resolution history for refinement. To address task interference during distillation, we separate specialist optimization from capability consolidation and introduce task-asymmetric on-policy distillation (TA-OPD). This procedure transfers prediction and refinement behaviors from specialized teachers into a shared student through local velocity matching under their respective sampling and history policies. {Extensive experiments demonstrate that ScaleWorld achieves high-quality world generation with improved computational and memory efficiency.
